     DESTRUCTION NOTICE

WHY —To prevent the enemy from using or salvaging this equipment for his benefit.

WHEN —When ordered by your commander.

HOW  —l1. Smash—wUse sledges, axes, handaxes, pickaxes, hammers, crowbars, heavy tools.
        2. Cut—Use axes, handaxes, machetes.
        8. Burn—Use gasoline, kerosene, oil, flame throwers, incendiary grenades,
        4, Explosives—Use firearms, grenades, TNT.
        5. Disposal—Bury in slit trenches, fox holes, other holes. Throw in streams. Scatter.

USE ANYTHING IMMEDIATELY AVAILABLE FOR DESTRUCTION OF THIS EQUIPMENT.

WHAT —1. Smash—Carrying case, amplifier, motor, carriage assembly, tubes.
                2. Cut—aAll wires, speaker cone, films, cables.
                  8. Burn—All smashed and cut material.
                4, Bury or scatter—All of the above material after burning.

     DESTROY EVERYTHING

                               PART ONE INTRODUCTION

                                     Section I. DESCRIPTION

1. General                                            (1) Recorder-Reproducers AN/GNQ-7 and
                                                  AN/GNQ-7A are very similar. Each has two
   a. Reproducer AN/GNQ-6 (figs. 1 and 2) isa     turntables which can be used to record on two
portable sound-reproducing equipment for tran-    disks simultaneously, or to record on one and
scribing from a disk record. The equipment re-    automatically continue the recording on the other
produces through a headset and is provided with   (with about a 60-second overlap period on each
a step-back and brake mechanism permitting con-   disk) and so on indefinitely by alternately placing
trol of the record while transcribing.            new disks on the turntables. Reproducing is done
                                                  on only one of the two turntables. Hereafter in
   b. Recorder-Reproducers AN/GNQ-7,              this manual the nomenclature AN/GNQ-7-(*)
AN/GNQ-7A, and AN/GNQ-8 (fig. 2) are port-        will refer to both models, and the specific nomen-
able sound-recording and _ sound-reproducing      clature AN/GNQ-7 or AN/GNQ-7A will be used
equipments designed for making 15-minute disk     only where the models differ.
recordings of dictations, conferences, telephone
conversations and the like, and playing back the     (2) Recorder-Reproducer AN/GNQ-8 has only
recordings at will. Each of these equipments re-  one turntable, on which both the recording and
produces through a loudspeaker and is provided    reproducing functions are accomplished.
with a logging system for determining recorded
positions on the record.
